Hello, Dr. Smith. Thank you so much for fitting me in, it's really wonderful to meet you too. I, um... I hope I'm not taking up too much of your time. I'm sure a lot of this is just normal stress—being a chief executive comes with a lot of pressure, and I always just want to make sure I'm doing a good job for everyone. 

But I guess I'm here today because... well, I've been experiencing something a little embarrassing, to be completely honest. I keep hearing people talking about me. Usually it's the board members or my staff. I'll hear them saying things like... like I'm ruining the company. And I hear it coming right through the conference room walls, or even from my cell phone when it's completely turned off. 

It's just that it sounds so incredibly clear to me that I actually answer them back out loud, right there in the room, before I even realize that... well, that no one else heard it. I'm so sorry, I know that probably sounds terrible and strange. Am I making sense? I just want to make sure I'm answering your question correctly.

Um... no, no doctor has ever mentioned anything like that to me. I've always been very healthy, thankfully. I try to take good care of myself, you know, eating right and staying active when I can. 

And as for medications or... or substances... oh goodness, no. I would never take anything that wasn't prescribed, and I haven't started any new medications recently. I know how important it is to keep a clear head, especially with my responsibilities at the gallery. I'd never do anything to jeopardize my work or my health. 

So, there's no medical reason or... or anything I've taken that would cause this. At least, nothing I know of. I hope that's a good thing? I mean, I want to be as helpful as possible so we can figure this out. Did I answer that the right way for you?

Oh... um... well, looking back before all of this really started... I suppose maybe things were already slipping a little bit. It's so hard to admit this, because I really pride myself on being such a go-getter. 

I used to be the person driving every single idea, you know? I was always the one calling people right after our gallery openings to follow up and keep the momentum going.
